# Fresh R4 analytical allocation: actual internal-block resolvents and reserve transfer

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926; original target OPEN. ONE NEW native gpt-6-astra/ultra invocation, consult OFF. ONE growing internal-auxiliary mechanism, at most TWO explicit laws; mathematical programs/array reads/solvers/scans and formal submissions ZERO. Parent owns analytical ledger. Read current OPERATOR.md and original PROBLEM.md. Old grants remain CLOSED; no automatic renewal, additional agents/models, workers/services or private reads.

## Original target through an accepted representation

For each fixed enclosing m>=1536, use epsilon=1/(4m),beta=1-epsilon,c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon),a=beta/c in every child of H0empty,H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Let S_j=sum H_j,M_j=1+2S_j. For one arbitrary compact convex D containing zero in any finite dimension, actual updates are x_(i+1)=x_i-h_i P_D(x_i). Define U_t(x)=max_(g in D)(2<x,g>-t||g||^2),t>0.

Seek an explicit all-depth proof U_1(T_Hm x)<=17 U_Mm(x)/16 for all bodies and starts. Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 gives finite-word projection-simplex universality with the ENTIRE polar minimum set and exact actual H3 U propagation for trailing d>=1. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 gives B_(2^m-1)/M_m<47/50. Together with a true U bound they would imply original full-class liminf ratio<=799/800. This remains conditional; no exponent/finite/restricted-body result completes the target. W=2R, same physical m throughout.

## Completed failure and genuine changed hypothesis

Read research/multiscale_auxiliary_search_20261008_r3/REPORT.md, SHAe3570d96fdcf058207962186d2be0084a5ac35c82d19161340fce21484507c90, HANDOFF.md and MAIN_READBACK.md. In the primitive row recursion the two fixed e,b/b,e entries occur once per join, not once per right query.

That call used all right-suffix entrance projections a_j=P_D(x_(2^m-2^j)/M_j), and then an all-initial-scale chain q_j=P_D(x0/M_j). Both complete laws fail on g16..g19=v,g24..g27=-v,allotherg0,x0=0: physical displacement cancels, and ALL their auxiliary inputs/reserves vanish. The complete formal residual is K4(1+K3)R2||v||^2/M_m<0. This is not a legal trajectory and does not exclude the target. It DOES exclude repairing that same base only with auxiliary energies vanishing on those existing zero coordinates.

The new mechanism must add actual inputs at INTERNAL block entrances/exits that see these two defects separately, or genuinely change their physical-auxiliary coupling. Do not return another initial-only chain or right-spine-only reserve. No auxiliary point may be selected independently of its specified input or from a different body.

## Concrete available local identities

Read the accepted research/projection_simplex_h3_selected_gate_20261003/PROOF.md Part B, especially sections4-6, including all coefficients and all original normal rows. A left block of mass s, input x, exit v, physical bridge1+k to w=v-(1+k)g with g=P_D(v), C=1+s,h=C+k, trailing T>0,M=T+2h, p=P_D(x/M),r=P_D(w/T),zbar=[x-w+(T+h)r]/M has the exact identity

U_M(x)-U_T(w)
 = [V_left+Theta||g-r||^2+2h(1+C/(T+k))Np+2h*C/(T+k)Nr]/M + Rem,

where V_left=||x||^2-||v+s*g||^2,Theta=h[C*T-k(C+T+k)]/(T+k),Np=<v-g,g-r>,Nr=<w-T*r,r-g>,Rem=M||p-zbar||^2+2<x-Mp,p-zbar>. The primitive term stays SIGNED unless paid by an actual proved identity. The accepted H3 proof closes only with primitive H2 children.

Read main_radial_auxiliary_reserve_20261008_r3.md. For actual g=P_D(z),r=P_D(z/d),d>1,delta=g-r, N1=<z-g,delta>,N2=<z-dr,-delta>,

Lrad(s,z,d)=[2sd+s^2(d+1)]/(d-1)||delta||^2
 +2s(d+s)/(d-1)N1+2s(1+s)/(d-1)N2.

Both N are nonnegative original normals. The identity was independently checked but remains an unsubmitted draft. At d1,delta0 and Lrad0; do not use singular coefficients there.

## At most two all-internal designs

Choose at most TWO complete coefficient/reserve laws within a growing dyadic auxiliary tree. One concrete schedule of actual auxiliary inputs uses EVERY dyadic block B=[ell,r) of mass S_B, its actual entrance x_ell and exit x_r, and the physical trailing mass after r. For example d_B=1+2sum_(t>=r)h_t yields actual entrance/exit resolvents P_D(x_ell/(d_B+2S_B)) and P_D(x_r/d_B). All input vectors must be explicitly expanded in original x0 and physical gradients; d_B is a coefficient scale, not a restarted trajectory. This is a proposed geometry, not a sufficient law.

Alternatively introduce a second explicit family of scales at the internal H2/H3 boundaries, using their actual finite-block U identities and radial/maximum remainders. Substituting a child U identity for primitive V may introduce NEGATIVE maximization/radial terms; these must be retained and paid, not discarded. Specify every original augmented normal coefficient, every duplicate-label identification, the entire residual and all boundary terms. No unknown child PSD or unproved reserve can be an induction premise.

An energy theorem is usable only after the actual coefficient graph is matched. SOURCE_TREE_EMBEDDING_20261008_R3.md records a primary tree-Carleson theorem with factor4, but its positive weights, subtree averages, boundary conditions and constant are NOT matched to this GD problem. Do not treat an analogy or a Hardy/Schur formula as the requested payment. The existing negative zero-aggregate assignment is an early screen, not the only test of a complete matrix.

## Deliverable and stop

Write REPORT/HANDOFF in this new output directory only. Provide a complete all-depth original-normal/SOS certificate with the requested constant, or an exact obstruction to each actual new law with its complete scope. A formal negative assignment is not a realizable GD function. If the remaining payment has theorem-level strength, state it explicitly and do not label it technical or progress.

Only text reads, manual analysis, Markdown writing and byte hashes. No mathematical scripts or calculator use, recurrence/symbolic replays, numerical arrays or diagnostics, additional models/agents, paid consults, installs, worker/service actions, truth-store edits, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown or Goal mutation. # R10 inverse PP joint attainability handoff

**UNVERIFIED. Original target OPEN. Allocation a790e207527be496 exhausted and stopped.**

One assigned gpt-6-astra/ultra invocation; two substantive analytical approaches; mathematical programs/arrays/solvers/formal submissions/further agents/further models all 0. Parent owns budget and shared administration. Only this packet's PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md were written. Token metering is not exposed to the child.

The report retains the exact all-word target lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, W=2R, all integer benchmark splits, original minimizer/radius and actual last iterate. It supplies no complete target-level candidate.

## Actual all-depth results

Set sigma=1+sqrt(2), m>=1536, eps=1/(4m), beta=1-eps, c=sigma(1+eps), a=beta/c and H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j), with these SAME parameters in every child. Let B=B_(2^m-1), D=1+2 sum(H_m), Q=(B+1)/2. Both policies start backward from the benchmark terminal law +/-Q, whose actual twice-value is B. Atom splits duplicate all fixed future data on one static projection simplex.

1. **Exact one-step variance minimization fails even at benchmark terminal scale.** At every step >2, the actual optimal cap selects a fraction theta=d/Delta of the minimum atom and multiplies support diameter by 1+1/d. Across the complete inherited hierarchy, the total removed bottom mass is <700/Q<1/4; this explicit bootstrap keeps every cap feasible. Every lowest cap has 2<d<5/2. There are 2^(m-3) such caps. The original variance therefore exceeds Q*(7/5)^(2^(m-3)-1), and the policy's actual payoff is <2*(7/5)^(-2^(m-3)+1). Its ratio to 1/B tends to 0. This strengthens the old independent-policy failure; it does NOT equate greedy local variance with globally minimal prefix cost.

2. **Primitive-potential look-ahead with a symmetric two-point endpoint remains exactly Huber.** For cost Var+2(C-1)F+C(C-1)||P||^2, bridge excess d<C, and next law +/-q, put gamma=d(C+d)/(C-d). If q>gamma, the unique inverse optimizer is the nonbranching law +/-(q+h). At every inherited level this condition holds both for the true benchmark capacity C_i and, by a separate estimate, for actual prefix sum+1. For benchmark C_i, the exact check is gamma/Q < 2(3sigma+4)/(3sigma(3sigma-4)) < 1, where (3sigma)^2-6(3sigma)-8=1. Thus the whole physical word stays on one Huber ray, has original radius squared (Q+sum H_m)^2, and its B-normalized payoff is <(94/97)^2=1-573/9409, using the accepted B/D<47/50.

The actual prefix sum+1 can be below sigma^j at small inherited levels; its valid separate estimate is d/C<=c-2<3/7. The report does not conflate those capacity bounds.

## Scope and recovery boundary

These are exact failures of two specified real-law policies on a complete unbounded hierarchy, not upper bounds on W(H_m) or a rejection of the full inverse family. They do not show that the same terminal two-point law is insufficient under the true globally optimized whole-prefix cost. They also leave multi-value terminal laws, coupled boundary masses, and other fixed projection bodies open.

The generic PP cost identity and root coefficient match are not completion. A next construction must change the actual intermediate-law or prefix-cost mechanism enough to escape both the cap tail explosion and the flat-face Huber invariant, and must ultimately give uniform all-word original-radius control.

The parent's variable-wall note was intentionally not opened or attempted, to respect the two-approach cap. It is a separate later-allocation lead. No new allowance is requested or consumed here.

Read PROOF_ATTEMPT.md for the finite-law construction, the complete bottom-mass bootstrap, every constant, the original-radius calculation, and the distinction between actual witness payoff and the full worst-case value. New results remain UNVERIFIED; no formal submission is warranted as a complete target proof.

# R10: two actual inverse policies on the complete inherited hierarchy

**Status: UNVERIFIED direct mathematical derivation. The original minimax target remains OPEN.**

Allocation `a790e207527be496`; one native research invocation, at most two substantive approaches. This report uses exactly two approaches, no mathematical program, numerical array, solver, formal submission, extra agent, or extra model. Only this packet is written. The variable-wall extension sent by the parent was not opened or attempted after the two approaches were allocated.

The outcome is a pair of explicit all-depth obstructions to concrete policies. Neither is an upper bound on the full worst-case value of gradient descent, and neither excludes optimized whole-word inversion with a different terminal shape. There is no complete candidate to submit for independent verification.

## 1. Governing target and sources

The required affirmative result is: for every epsilon > 0 there is N_epsilon, independent of the word, such that every n >= N_epsilon and every predetermined h in [0,infinity)^n satisfy

\[
 \mathcal W_n(h)\ge (1-\epsilon)/B_n,
 \qquad \mathcal W_n(h)=\sup\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_*)}{L\|x_0-x_*\|^2}.
\]

Equivalently, the original target is lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1, with U_n^ZJ = 1/B_n and W = 2R. A negative resolution requires a full-function-class asymptotic upper certificate. Every original minimizer, original radius, physical step, and actual last iterate must be retained.

The exact capacities use every integer split k+l=n-1:

\[
 C_0=B_0=1,\quad
 C_n=\max\Omega(C_k,C_l),\quad B_n=\max G(C_k,B_l),
\]
\[
 \Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,
 \qquad G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2.
\]

Read in full: OPERATOR.md, the original PROBLEM.md, this packet's TASK.md, R9_DIRECT_HANDOFF_20261009.md, and R9_PARENT_INVERSE_COST_NOTE_20261009.md. The TASK SHA-256 was checked as `44e039d09f7f41336d95709e1a44b2333842f09a60356fe2469bace6bbdf0f66`; the R9 parent note was checked as `a22724c1260b4cfd6f9738f09b419d0a52f07d43cb12fe92eff77f1effd832d0`.

The finite-law geometry and inverse identities below are reconstructed from those supplied sources. The only accepted-project quantitative input used for the failure comparison is the task's fact `931b9653ce0f7201`: on the complete inherited hierarchy below, B_(n_m)/D_m < 47/50 for m >= 1536. Its verifier status is supplied by the governing handoff; it was not reverified in this invocation. No all-word converse is imported from Zhang--Jiang.

## 2. One function and the exact inverse operations

On a finite probability space with strictly positive weights, use the Euclidean Hilbert space

\[
 E=\{X:\mathbb EX=0\},\qquad
 \|X\|^2=\mathbb EX^2,
 \qquad K=\{g\in E:g_i\ge-1\}.
\]

The coordinate map X_i -> sqrt(p_i) X_i identifies E with an ordinary finite-dimensional Euclidean subspace. The function

\[
 F(X)=\max_{g\in K}\{\mathbb E[Xg]-\tfrac12\mathbb E g^2\}
\]

is convex and differentiable with gradient P_K X. The projection is nonexpansive, hence F is globally 1-smooth. Also F >= 0 and F(0)=0. Since 0 lies in the relative interior of K, the only X in E with P_K X=0 is X=0. Thus the selected minimizer is the original point 0, uniquely.

The actual projection and update are

\[
 g=(X-t)_+-1,\qquad \mathbb E(X-t)_+=1,
 \qquad Y=X-hg.
\]

In the inverse formulas, a denotes the threshold parameter; the separately scoped hierarchy parameter in Section 3 is named a as in the task. For h=1+d>1, choose a >= max(d,max Y), put Z=a-Y, and select conditional probabilities omega in [0,1] with E[omega Z]=d. Replace each atom by the two preimages

\[
 X_L=Y-h=a-h-Z,\qquad
 X_R=a-h+Z/d.
 \tag{2.1}
\]

Their probabilities are the old probability times 1-omega and omega. Their mean is zero. At threshold a-h their positive excess has mean E[omega Z]/d=1, so (2.1) gives the actual projection. Both branches map exactly to the old Y. Zero-weight branches are omitted. Duplicating every already-built future value on each split preserves all future thresholds, gradients, and objectives. After finitely many splits there is one fixed finite function and one fixed full physical trajectory.

These branches also exhaust the preimages for this fixed simplex geometry. Any physical threshold t obeys t>=-1, since 1=E(X-t)_+>=-t. On either branch the output is at most t+h; thus a=t+h>=max(d,max Y). Solving the inactive and active update formulas gives exactly (2.1), and mean zero gives E[omega Z]=d.

Writing J=E[omega Z^2], direct expansion gives

\[
 V(X)=V(Y)+2ha-h^2+(d^{-2}-1)J.
 \tag{2.2}
\]

More generally, for A,B >= 0,

\[
 \mathcal C_{A,B}(X)=V(X)+2AF(X)+B\|P_KX\|^2
\]
\[
 =V(Y)+2(h+A)a-h^2-2Ah+A-B
   +\left(\frac{1+A+B}{d^2}-1\right)J.
 \tag{2.3}
\]

All costs in this report are computed at the original minimizer. No physical rescaling of a child is used.

### 2.1 Exact facts about minimizing (2.2)

At fixed a, minimizing J fills the highest Y values first; maximizing J fills the lowest Y values first. This follows because the amount of constraint filled by an atom is omega(a-Y), while its contribution to J divided by that amount is a-Y. There is at most one fractional cutoff atom, so a fixed-step minimizer requires at most one extra atom.

For a cutoff value z and the fully selected set A, on each interval with the same cutoff,

\[
 J'(a)=d+\mathbb E[1_A(z-Y)].
 \tag{2.4}
\]

For 1<h<2, the minimizing allocation is the upper tail. Consequently

\[
 \frac{d}{da}\min_\omega V(X)
 =\frac{h^2}{d}-(d^{-2}-1)\mathbb E(Y-z)_+.
 \tag{2.5}
\]

Define

\[
 \gamma_h=\frac{hd}{1-d}=\frac{h(h-1)}{2-h}.
 \tag{2.6}
\]

If a minimizing allocation used a positive fraction of a minimum atom y_min, then z=y_min and E(Y-z)_+=-y_min. If -y_min > gamma_h, (2.5) is strictly negative. Increasing a slightly would reduce the cost while that fraction remains positive. Therefore **no minimizing allocation selects the minimum atom whenever -y_min > gamma_h and max Y>d**. This conclusion includes breakpoint minimizers: a minimum atom with zero selected fraction remains entirely on the left branch.

For h>2, the minimizing allocation is the lower tail. In (2.4),

\[
 0\le \mathbb E[1_A(z-Y)]\le d,
\]

because z<=a and the selected constraint mass is d. Thus d<=J'(a)<=2d. Since d^{-2}-1<0, the derivative of the optimized cost is at least

\[
 2h+(d^{-2}-1)2d=2+2/d>0.
\]

Hence the optimal threshold is always a=max(d,max Y). If M=max Y>d and the minimum atom has enough capacity, the optimum selects only that minimum atom and takes a=M.

These are one-step optimization statements, not statements about the least original radius over a whole prefix.

## 3. The complete inherited hierarchy and useful elementary bounds

Fix any m>=1536, and keep these same enclosing parameters in every child:

\[
 \sigma=1+\sqrt2,\quad \epsilon=1/(4m),\quad
 \beta=1-\epsilon,\quad c=\sigma(1+\epsilon),\quad a=\beta/c,
\]
\[
 H_0=\varnothing,\qquad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j).
\]

Let n_m=2^m-1, S_j=sum H_j, D_m=1+2S_m, and abbreviate B=B_(n_m). The terminal law in both approaches is

\[
 Y_n=\pm Q\quad\hbox{with equal probability},
 \qquad Q=(B+1)/2.
 \tag{3.1}
\]

Here Q>1, the terminal projection is +/-1, and 2F(Y_n)=2Q-1=B.

The following estimates use no numerical calculation.

First, C_(2^j-1)>=sigma^j follows by balanced splits. Also B_n>=C_n: indeed Omega(x,y)<=2x+y<=G(x,y), and induction uses monotonicity of G. Therefore

\[
 B\ge G(C_{n_{m-1}},C_{n_{m-1}})
 =4C_{n_{m-1}}\ge4\sigma^{m-1},
 \qquad Q>2\sigma^{m-1}>2^m.
 \tag{3.2}
\]

Second,

\[
 (1+1/(4m))^m<\sum_{k\ge0}(1/4)^k=4/3.
 \tag{3.3}
\]

The largest bridge excess is d_max=a c^(m-1)=beta c^(m-2), whence

\[
 \frac{d_{\max}}{Q}<\frac{2}{3\sigma}<\frac13.
 \tag{3.4}
\]

The steps at levels 0 and 1 are h_0=1+a<sqrt(2) and h_1=1+beta=2-epsilon. Their gamma values satisfy gamma_(h_0)<1 and gamma_(h_1)<8m. Every step at level at least 2 is >2. Its smallest excess is

\[
 d_{\rm low}=a c^2=\beta c=\sigma(1-\epsilon^2),
 \qquad 2<d_{\rm low}<5/2.
 \tag{3.5}
\]

The upper bound c<5/2 is immediate for m>=1536, for example from sqrt(2)<17/12. We also have the stronger c<17/7 used later.

Finally, c(c-2)>1>beta, so 0<a/(c-2)<1. Solving the exact sum recurrence gives

\[
 S_j+1=\frac{a}{c-2}c^j+
       \left(1-\frac{a}{c-2}\right)2^j,
 \qquad S_j<c^j-1\quad(j\ge1).
 \tag{3.6}
\]

In particular D_m<2c^m. The accepted comparison used below is

\[
 B/D_m<47/50.
 \tag{3.7}
\]

## 4. Approach 1: minimize the true one-step variance at every reverse step

### 4.1 Policy and statement

Start at (3.1), process the complete physical word H_m backward, and at each step minimize the actual predecessor variance (2.2) over all admissible a and omega. Choose an extreme fractional-knapsack allocation; aggregate equal-value atoms. The optimum exists: for 1<h<2 the objective is continuous piecewise affine and eventually has positive slope, while for h>2 its minimum is at the lower endpoint as shown above. Any remaining threshold tie can be resolved by taking the least minimizing a.

This is a fully specified finite-law construction, with at most n_m+2 atoms after all splits. The following bound is obtained for its actual payoff, denoted P_G(m):

\[
 V_0>Q(7/5)^{\,2^{m-3}-1},
 \qquad
 P_G(m)=\frac{B}{V_0}
 <2(7/5)^{-\,2^{m-3}+1}.
 \tag{4.1}
\]

Consequently

\[
 B P_G(m)
 <\frac{16}{3}\sigma^m(7/5)^{-\,2^{m-3}+1}
 \longrightarrow0.
 \tag{4.2}
\]

This is a failure of this actual witness policy, even with the benchmark-scaled terminal law. It does not bound W_(n_m)(H_m) from above.

### 4.2 Exact cap dynamics

Write the current minimum and maximum as y_min and M, their separation as Delta=M-y_min, and the probability of the minimum as p_min.

At a step h=1+d>2, suppose M>d and d/Delta<p_min. The unique optimal threshold is a=M and the lower-tail allocation selects only the minimum value. The total probability sent to the right branch is

\[
 \theta=d/\Delta.
\]

The new minimum and maximum are respectively

\[
 y_{\min}'=y_{\min}-h,\qquad
 M'=M-h+\Delta/d.
\]

The remaining minimum probability is p_min-theta, and

\[
 \boxed{\Delta'=(1+1/d)\Delta.}
 \tag{4.3}
\]

All other atoms move left by h. The new maximum has mass theta. Thus a small removed bottom mass creates a very distant upper atom. The next physical laws remain exactly the previously fixed laws.

At either of the two smaller step types, as long as the minimum is <=-Q, Section 2.1 shows it is never selected: Q>8m exceeds both gamma values. It moves left by h with unchanged mass. Every old maximum has some preimage at least M-h, irrespective of which branch is used. Thus

\[
 \Delta'\ge\Delta
 \tag{4.4}
\]

at a smaller step.

### 4.3 Bottom-mass bootstrap over the entire word

The word H_m is a palindrome. Reading it backward gives the same hierarchy. There are

\[
 N=2^{m-2}-1
\]

steps >2. In their chronological order within the reversed word, indexed k=1,...,N, their excesses are

\[
 d_k=\beta c^{1+v_2(k)}.
 \tag{4.5}
\]

In particular every odd k is a lowest cap with d_k=d_low. Since c<5/2<4,

\[
 d_k<(5/2)k^2.
 \tag{4.6}
\]

Assume the formulas have applied through the preceding caps. Equations (4.3)--(4.4), the initial separation 2Q, and d_low<5/2 give, before cap k,

\[
 \Delta_k\ge2Q(7/5)^{\lfloor(k-1)/2\rfloor}.
 \tag{4.7}
\]

Therefore its prospective removed mass satisfies

\[
 \theta_k=\frac{d_k}{\Delta_k}
 <\frac5{4Q}\,k^2(5/7)^{\lfloor(k-1)/2\rfloor}.
\]

The entire infinite majorant is finite and explicit:

\[
 \sum_{k\ge1} k^2(5/7)^{\lfloor(k-1)/2\rfloor}
 =\sum_{j\ge0}(8j^2+12j+5)(5/7)^j
 =1085/2.
\]

Hence

\[
 \sum_k\theta_k<\frac{5425}{8Q}<\frac{700}{Q}<\frac14.
 \tag{4.8}
\]

This closes the bootstrap, rather than presupposing feasibility. Before every step the surviving minimum mass exceeds 1/4. The minimum equals -Q minus the sum of all already inverted steps. Mean zero then gives

\[
 M\ge\frac{p_{\min}(-y_{\min})}{1-p_{\min}}
 \ge Q/3>d_{\max}.
 \tag{4.9}
\]

Thus the cap threshold is indeed M. At the next cap, its prospective theta is less than 1/4 by (4.8), so it is less than the available minimum mass. The cap formula applies, and the same cumulative bound leaves the minimum mass greater than 1/4 afterward. At a smaller step, Q>8m and M>d permit Section 2.1, which leaves that mass unchanged. Induction covers the complete word.

No minimum is exhausted. In particular (4.3) is a real law update at every cap, not a recurrence assumed without feasible weights.

### 4.4 Original radius and actual payoff

There are 2^(m-3) lowest caps. Before the last cap,

\[
 \Delta\ge2Q(7/5)^{2^{m-3}-1}.
\]

That last cap has d=d_low>2. After it, the surviving minimum has mass >1/4, the new maximum has mass theta=d/Delta, and their separation is (1+1/d)Delta. The pairwise variance identity gives

\[
 V\ge p_{\min}'\theta\bigl((1+1/d)\Delta\bigr)^2
 >\frac{d\Delta}{4}>\frac\Delta2.
 \tag{4.10}
\]

The remaining reversed suffix is H_2, whose three steps all lie in (0,2). Such an inverse cannot decrease the variance: projection optimality against 0 gives <X,P_K X>>=||P_K X||^2, and therefore

\[
 V(X)-V(X-hP_KX)
 \ge h(2-h)\|P_KX\|^2\ge0.
\]

Thus (4.10) lower-bounds the original variance V_0 and proves (4.1). Since B/Q<2, B<D_m<2c^m, and c^m<(4/3)sigma^m, it also proves (4.2).

The decisive failure mechanism is not a lack of feasible inverse laws. All weights and all physical iterates exist. Repeated lowest caps retain almost all the original bottom mass while their rare upper atoms force exponentially growing support and original variance.

## 5. Approach 2: retain the primitive prefix potential and propagate a flat upper face

The first approach cannot treat local variance as whole-prefix cost. This second approach changes the optimized objective to the exact primitive-form potential. Its proposed invariant is a symmetric two-point endpoint, for which the whole prefix has a directly realizable constant-gradient completion. The invariant does propagate, but on H_m it propagates precisely the deficient Huber trajectory.

### 5.1 Exact inverse minimizer on a two-point law

For any capacity C>=1, use

\[
 A=C-1,\qquad B_{\rm cost}=C(C-1).
\]

Here B_cost is a local coefficient and is not B_n. Consider a physical step h=1+d with 0<d<C, and a next law Y=+/-q with equal probabilities. Put

\[
 w=C^2/d^2-1>0,\quad T=C+d,\quad
 \gamma=\frac{d(C+d)}{C-d}=d+2T/w.
\]

Assume q>gamma. In particular q>d, so a>=q. At fixed a, (2.3) selects the upper tail. The optimized J is explicitly

\[
 J(a)=
 \begin{cases}
 (d-q)a+dq+q^2,&q\le a\le q+2d,\\
 d(a-q),&a\ge q+2d.
 \end{cases}
 \tag{5.1}
\]

The slope of the full cost on the first interval is

\[
 2T+w(d-q)=w(\gamma-q)<0;
\]

on the second interval it is 2T+wd=w gamma>0. Hence the **unique minimizing threshold** is

\[
 a=q+2d.
\]

At this threshold the old positive atom is entirely on the right branch and the negative atom entirely on the left. There is no fractional split, and the actual predecessor law is exactly

\[
 \boxed{X=\pm(q+h)\quad\hbox{with equal probabilities}.}
 \tag{5.2}
\]

This is an attained, whole-law result. It uses the same cost coefficients that generate the PP root identity, and it shows when that objective unavoidably selects the nonbranching Huber inverse.

### 5.2 The benchmark capacity also stays in this regime at every inherited level

Index the physical steps by i=0,...,n_m-1. A step at level j has d=a c^j, and its prior prefix has length at least 2^j-1. The benchmark C_i is nondecreasing, since Omega(C_i,1)>C_i is an allowed next split. Thus

\[
 C_i\ge C_{2^j-1}\ge\sigma^j.
\]

By (3.3), the ratio at every such step obeys

\[
 \frac d{C_i}<\frac4{3\sigma}<1.
 \tag{5.3}
\]

Together with (3.4), this gives

\[
 \frac\gamma Q
 <\frac{2(3\sigma+4)}{3\sigma(3\sigma-4)}<1.
 \tag{5.4}
\]

The last strict inequality has an exact algebraic check. For x=3sigma, sigma^2=2sigma+1 gives x^2-6x-8=1>0, which is precisely x(x-4)>2(x+4).

Starting at q=Q, (5.2) increases q by each reversed physical step. Therefore q>=Q>gamma remains true at every stage. The policy that minimizes (2.3) using the true benchmark capacity C_i at each prefix consequently has the actual invariant

\[
 X_i=\pm\left(Q+\sum_{t=i}^{n_m-1}h_t\right).
 \tag{5.5}
\]

It never produces a new atom or a new active face. Evaluating the benchmark potential at a nonprimitive prefix is a policy choice here; it is **not** a proved representation of that prefix's globally minimum inverse radius. The all-step construction (5.5), however, is real and its radius is exact.

### 5.3 The actually realizable prefix-sum potential has the same outcome

For completeness, another natural capacity is C=1+P_i, where P_i is the actual sum of the preceding physical steps. It should not be claimed that C>=sigma^j: at small inherited levels that inequality can fail. Instead, the immediately preceding left child gives P_i>=S_j. Equation (3.6) yields

\[
 \frac{d}{1+P_i}\le\frac{a c^j}{S_j+1}\le c-2<3/7.
\]

Thus gamma<(5/2)d <=(5/2)d_max<Q, by (3.4). This policy therefore also obeys (5.5).

On every selected endpoint +/-r, its entire preceding word is physically realizable by a constant gradient, and the exact radius is

\[
 \mathcal C_{P_i,P_i(P_i+1)}(\pm r)
 =r^2+2P_i(r-1/2)+P_i(P_i+1)
 =(r+P_i)^2.
\]

This accounts for the entire prefix on the chosen invariant. It does not assert that the same scalar potential equals the least prefix radius for every nonflat law.

### 5.4 Exact fixed loss on the complete hierarchy

For either capacity choice, the one fixed two-atom function ends at +/-Q and starts at +/-(Q+S_m). Its actual payoff P_L(m) is

\[
 P_L(m)=\frac B{(Q+S_m)^2}.
\]

Since Q+S_m=(B+D_m)/2, (3.7) gives

\[
 \boxed{\quad
 B P_L(m)=\frac{4B^2}{(B+D_m)^2}
 <\left(\frac{94}{97}\right)^2
 =1-\frac{573}{9409}.
 \quad}
 \tag{5.6}
\]

This is a nonvanishing loss for every m>=1536. The inverse objective has changed materially from Approach 1 and avoids its rare-tail explosion, but it still fails the required benchmark on the complete inherited hierarchy.

## 6. What these attempts settle, and what remains open

Both policies have one static finite-dimensional convex 1-smooth function, a valid original minimizer, genuine nonnegative predetermined steps, exact original-radius accounting, and the actual final objective. Their failure is established on the complete hierarchy with inherited parameters, rather than on a fixed horizon or a child with silently refreshed parameters.

Approach 1 rules out the proposed invariant that greedy one-step variance minimization at benchmark terminal scale keeps the support and original cost controlled. Equations (4.3), (4.8), and (4.10) are the decisive actual-law inequalities. The obstruction applies even though every local minimization is exact.

Approach 2 rules out the proposed two-point/flat-face closure when its inverse choices minimize either the benchmark primitive-form potential or the physically realizable prefix-sum potential. Equations (5.1)--(5.5) derive the invariant and its cost; (5.6) gives its all-depth failure. In particular the PP root coefficient match, even combined with a correctly scaled terminal two-point law, does not make this closure work.

Neither argument proves that the two-point terminal law fails under the true globally optimal whole-prefix cost. That cost may differ materially from both objectives used here on intermediate nonflat laws. Nor do these arguments exclude terminal laws with several selected values, full-word-dependent boundary masses, or a different fixed projection body. Those are unexamined alternatives, not claims proved viable in this allocation.

The missing object remains an actual whole-word compatible construction with a uniform original-radius bound, not another identity for the coefficients. # R5 handoff

UNVERIFIED analytical draft. Original exact GD minimax target OPEN. No complete candidate was obtained.

One new invocation, two substantive proof lines, mathematical PROGRAM0, numerical/array/solver0, formal0, no additional agent/model. Only the two requested output files in this new directory were written. Parent owns registration and closeout.

## Actual output

PROOF_ATTEMPT.md contains the full definitions, functions, trajectories, radii, cross-history inequalities and unresolved original-target implication.

The support-clock recurrence in the supplied note is correct for one actually realized child. The first line retains its missing minimum height:

\[
M=\max_j\langle v_0,b_j\rangle,\qquad
H=\min_j\langle v_j-b_j,b_j\rangle.
\]

For prefix mass \(C-1\) and bridge \(1+d\), a static orthogonal prefix-to-child hull needs no auxiliary clock when \(H/M\ge1-d/C\). Its added original squared radius is exactly \(4CM\), and its payoff is \(z_B/(a_B+4C)\). Every old and reverse row is checked. A single Huber child jointly attains the height, support and value needed to match the Zhang--Jiang bridge exactly. But the operation divides the height/support ratio by its support growth factor, so that local equality is not a recursive closure theorem.

The second line constructs a full static simplex with negative cross inner products. For any chosen phase partition with checkpoints exceeding 1, its vectors are explicit, every history row holds, its minimizer is uniquely 0, and its exact original-radius payoff is equation (17). Its radius includes a genuine subtractive centering term, rather than an unaccounted auxiliary coordinate. This is a realized whole-word function, not just a formal Gram direction.

## Exact unresolved point

Neither line proves that every word surviving the global Huber and all scalar quadratic baselines admits parameters and a partition reaching \((1-o(1))/B_n\), uniformly in the word and with the exact integer-split benchmark. No recursively attainable support/height state was postulated. No restricted-family failure on an easy Silver word was used to reject the full baseline-plus-pasting pool.

The remaining selection-and-payoff inequality has target-level strength. It is not a routine interpolation gap. No negative answer follows from its absence.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-002542 -->
# R5: joint histories, retained heights, and a static simplex replacement

2026-10-08. UNVERIFIED analytical draft. Original target OPEN.

This was one new analytical invocation with two substantive lines. No mathematical program, numerical array, solver, additional model, or formal submission was used. The constructions below are actual functions with complete trajectories. No proof that their portfolio attains the required uniform lower bound was obtained, and no full-class negative resolution was obtained.

## 1. Exact target and the baseline pool

Set \(L=1\). For the prescribed nonnegative word \(h\), write \(S=\sum h_i\) and

\[
W_n(h)=\sup_{f,x_0,x_\star}
\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad x_{i+1}=x_i-h_i\nabla f(x_i).
\]

The supremum has the full finite-dimensional smooth-convex scope in PROBLEM.md. Define \(C_0=B_0=1\) and, over every integer split \(k+l=n-1\),

\[
C_n=\max\Omega(C_k,C_l),\qquad B_n=\max G(C_k,B_l),
\]

\[
\Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,\qquad
G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2.
\]

The missing conclusion is

\[
\forall\epsilon>0\ \exists N_\epsilon\
\forall n\ge N_\epsilon\ \forall h\in[0,\infty)^n:
\quad W_n(h)\ge(1-\epsilon)/B_n,
\]

with a threshold independent of the word.

Two actual baseline witnesses are retained throughout this attempt:

\[
W_n(h)\ge \frac1{2S+1},
\qquad
W_n(h)\ge \lambda\prod_{i=0}^{n-1}(1-h_i\lambda)^2
\quad(0\le\lambda\le1).
\tag{1}
\]

The first is the Huber function with derivative \(\operatorname{clip}(x,-1,1)\), started at \(2S+1\). Every query remains on its positive affine ray. The second is \(f(x)=\lambda x^2/2\), started at 1. Both use the original last iterate and radius. Thus a word needing further work must fail both selected lower bounds at the proposed target level. The R4 ceiling on a particular pasted quadratic-Silver family was not used to exclude this larger pool.

The source motivating the joint boundary quantities is Zhang--Jiang, [v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), Sections 2.2, 3.1 and 3.2. Its sufficient composition theorems were not reversed or treated as a classification of arbitrary words.

## 2. Line 1: exact support bookkeeping and the retained child heights

### 2.1 Static realization convention

For a compact finite gradient hull \(K\) containing 0, use

\[
F_K(x)=\max_{g\in K}\{\langle x,g\rangle-\tfrac12\|g\|^2\}.
\tag{2}
\]

Its gradient is \(P_Kx\), so it is convex and 1-smooth, with \(F_K(0)=0=\min F_K\). A proposed gradient \(g_i\in K\) at \(x_i\) is realized precisely when

\[
\langle x_i-g_i,g-g_i\rangle\le0\quad(g\in K).
\tag{3}
\]

Checking the generating vertices suffices. Values are then fixed as
\(\langle x_i,g_i\rangle-\|g_i\|^2/2\). Every use of (2) below selects one body before the run; no function is changed at a checkpoint.

### 2.2 Independent check of SUPPORT_CLOCK_RECURRENCE_20261008_R5

Take an actual suffix projection history \((v_j,b_j)\) for its prescribed word of mass \(\tau\). Let

\[
M=\max_j\langle v_0,b_j\rangle>0,\quad
R_B^2=\|v_0\|^2,\quad
a_B=R_B^2/M,\quad z_B=2F_B(v_{\rm end})/M.
\]

For the preceding prefix mass \(\sigma\), set \(C=1+\sigma\), and write the bridge as \(1+d>1\). Choose

\[
\theta>0,\quad r\ge0,\quad
\theta+(\tau+1)r\le d,\qquad
p^2=\frac{M}{\theta(1+r)},\quad T=(C+\theta)p.
\]

Use \(X_0=(v_0,T)\), prefix gradients \((0,p)\), and suffix gradients \((b_j,-rp)\). During the prefix its horizontal coordinate is \(v_0\); during the suffix its clock coordinate is \((\theta-d+t_jr)p\).

The prefix-to-suffix normal rows are bounded above by
\(M-\theta(1+r)p^2=0\). The reverse rows are

\[
-\langle v_j-b_j,b_j\rangle
-[d-\theta-(t_j+1)r](1+r)p^2\le0.
\]

The within-child and minimizer rows also hold. Thus the convex hull of 0 and these displayed gradients realizes the complete trajectory.

The initial support of the full body is exactly

\[
M_{\rm new}=Tp=JM,\qquad
J=\frac{C+\theta}{\theta(1+r)}.
\]

Indeed every suffix support is at most \(M-rTp<Tp\). Its actual state is therefore

\[
a_{\rm new}=C+\theta+\frac{a_B}{J},
\]

\[
z_{\rm new}=\frac{z_B}{J}
+\frac{2r(d-\theta)-(2\tau+1)r^2}{C+\theta}.
\tag{4}
\]

The selected-origin payoff is \(z_{\rm new}/a_{\rm new}\). The recurrence is correct, but each input is a property of one and the same realized child. It is not legitimate to optimize \(a_B,z_B,M\) separately and feed the resulting values into (4).

### 2.3 A pasting paid by actual heights, without an auxiliary clock

The full R4 normal rows contain a useful quantity that (4) does not retain. For the same actual child define

\[
H=\min_j\langle v_j-b_j,b_j\rangle\ge0,\qquad \eta=H/M.
\tag{5}
\]

For a nontrivial child, \(0\le\eta<1\): its initial row has
\(\langle v_0-b_0,b_0\rangle\le M-\|b_0\|^2<M\).

Choose \(v>0\) and \(a>0\) satisfying

\[
a^2=M/v,\qquad H\ge(1-d/v)M.
\tag{6}
\]

Use one new scalar coordinate for a constant prefix gradient, with original start

\[
X_0=((C+v)a,v_0).
\]

For prefix partial mass \(s_i\), prescribe

\[
X_i=((C+v-s_i)a,v_0),\qquad g_i=(a,0).
\]

The bridge lands at \(((v-d)a,v_0)\). For the entire suffix prescribe

\[
Y_j=((v-d)a,v_j),\qquad g_j=(0,b_j).
\]

Take the single body

\[
K=\operatorname{conv}(\{0,(a,0)\}\cup\{(0,b_j)\}).
\tag{7}
\]

Every prefix-to-suffix row is at most

\[
\langle v_0,b_j\rangle-(\sigma-s_i+v)a^2
\le M-va^2=0.
\]

Every reverse row is

\[
(v-d)a^2-\langle v_j-b_j,b_j\rangle\le0
\]

by (6). The child rows and all rows against 0 hold. Consequently (2),(7) realize exactly the displayed GD trajectory, including all old values and gradients of the suffix. The prefix values are explicitly \((\sigma-s_i+v+1/2)a^2\). Its terminal value is precisely the child terminal value.

The original radius is

\[
\|X_0\|^2=R_B^2+\frac{(C+v)^2}{v}M.
\tag{8}
\]

This is a valid selected-minimizer lower witness; no claim that 0 is always the closest minimizer is needed for this lower bound.

The particularly useful choice \(v=C\) is legal when

\[
\eta\ge1-d/C.
\tag{9}
\]

Its added squared radius is exactly \(4CM\), giving

\[
W(h_{\rm prefix},1+d,h_{\rm suffix})
\ge\frac{z_B}{a_B+4C}.
\tag{10}
\]

The state after this actual pasting is also explicit. Its initial support is
\(M_{\rm new}=JM\), \(J=1+C/v\). Its minimum height is \(H\), since the minimum prefix height is \(M\). Thus

\[
a_{\rm new}=C+v+a_B/J,\quad
z_{\rm new}=z_B/J,\quad
\eta_{\rm new}=\eta/J.
\tag{11}
\]

Unlike the clock specialization with \(r=0\), this operation permits \(v>d\) when the child's genuine positive height pays the reverse rows. But (11) also shows why the height condition cannot silently be inherited unchanged.

### 2.4 The Zhang--Jiang bridge is locally tight for this actual construction

This is a check of the construction, not a global converse. Put \(T_0=2\tau+1\), let \(d>0\) solve

\[
2d^2+T_0d=CT_0,\qquad
B=2C+2d+T_0=G(C,T_0).
\]

Then \(A=T_0+2d=\sqrt{BT_0}\). Take an actual constant-gradient Huber suffix with gradient \(b\), start \(v_0=Ab\), and its prescribed nonnegative suffix steps. Its whole trajectory stays on the ray, because \(A\ge\tau+1\). Direct calculation gives

\[
a_B=A,\quad z_B=2-T_0/A,\quad
\eta=1-(\tau+1)/A.
\]

These jointly attained values satisfy

\[
Bz_B-a_B=4C,\qquad
d/C=T_0/A\ge(\tau+1)/A=1-\eta.
\]

Thus (9) holds and (10) attains \(1/B\) exactly. No favorable support or height was postulated independently of the child.

This tight local match does not settle the hard words. For an arbitrary suffix, the Huber state depends on its actual mass, while the desired comparison uses the length-based exact \(B_l\). If a different adverse child is selected to improve its objective, (9) still requires its own minimum height. Equation (11) can decrease that height ratio. I did not prove a jointly attainable family that resolves these constraints at every required split of every surviving word.

## 3. Line 2: a static simplex with negative cross inner products

The second line attacked a concrete cost in the first: independent positive coordinate gradients retain positive old supports that every future history must dominate. The following construction instead uses one centered simplex. It preserves all history with explicit negative cross inner products and pays a centered initial variance, not a sum of independent coordinate radii.

### 3.1 Construction for any chosen phase partition

Partition the whole prescribed word into \(m\ge2\) phases. For \(i<m\), phase \(i\) consists of any prefix subword of mass \(\sigma_i\), followed by a chosen checkpoint \(b_i>1\). The last phase has mass \(\tau\). Define

\[
C_i=1+\sigma_i,\quad d_i=b_i-1\quad(i<m),
\qquad C_m=1+\tau.
\]

The total mass is

\[
S=\sum_{i=1}^m C_i+\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}d_i-1.
\]

Choose positive numbers \(A_i\) such that

\[
\sum_{i=1}^m A_i^{-1}=1,\qquad
P_i:=\sum_{j=i}^m A_jC_j,\qquad
A_i d_i\ge P_{i+1}\quad(i<m).
\tag{12}
\]

Such numbers exist for every selected partition: first set a positive temporary \(A_m\), choose earlier \(A_i\) backwards sufficiently large to meet the displayed inequalities, and finally multiply all \(A_i\) by their temporary reciprocal sum. The inequalities are homogeneous and normalization then holds. This is an existence construction, not a numerical evaluation.

In \(\mathbb R^m\), put

\[
u=(A_1^{-1/2},\ldots,A_m^{-1/2}),\qquad
w_i=\sqrt{A_i}e_i-u.
\]

Then \(\|u\|=1\), all \(w_i\) lie in \(V=u^\perp\), and

\[
\langle w_i,w_j\rangle=-1\ (i\ne j),\qquad
\|w_i\|^2=A_i-1.
\tag{13}
\]

Moreover \(\sum_i A_i^{-1}w_i=0\), with strictly positive coefficients. These vectors span \(V\), and 0 is in the relative interior of their simplex.

Take the actual function (2) on the finite-dimensional space \(V\), with

\[
K=\operatorname{conv}\{w_1,\ldots,w_m\}.
\]

Its minimizer is uniquely 0 in \(V\). Define

\[
\bar P=\sum_i P_i/A_i,\qquad
X_0=\sum_i \frac{P_i}{\sqrt{A_i}}e_i-\bar P\,u.
\tag{14}
\]

This point lies in \(V\), and

\[
\langle X_0,w_i\rangle=P_i-\bar P,\qquad
R^2:=\|X_0\|^2=\sum_i P_i^2/A_i-\bar P^2>0.
\tag{15}
\]

The strict positivity follows because the \(P_i\) strictly decrease and (15) is their variance under the positive weights \(1/A_i\).

### 3.2 Every phase and cross-history row

Prescribe gradient \(w_i\) throughout phase \(i\), including its checkpoint query when \(i<m\). Let \(q_j\) be the accumulated step mass already spent with gradient \(w_j\), and let \(s=\sum_jq_j\) be the global accumulated mass. The physical GD recursion gives

\[
\langle X,w_j\rangle=P_j-\bar P+s-A_jq_j.
\]

Write \(z_j=P_j-A_jq_j\). For a query intended to use \(w_i\), its projection row against any other vertex is exactly

\[
\langle X-w_i,w_j-w_i\rangle=z_j-z_i+A_i.
\tag{16}
\]

For a future phase \(j>i\), \(z_j=P_j\le P_{i+1}\). During the prefix portion of phase \(i\),

\[
z_i-A_i\ge P_i-A_iC_i=P_{i+1}.
\]

For every finished earlier phase \(j<i\), the checkpoint has left

\[
z_j=P_j-A_j(C_j+d_j)=P_{j+1}-A_jd_j\le0.
\]

These inequalities prove (16) is nonpositive throughout the entire run. In the last phase the analogous terminal bound is \(z_m-A_m\ge0\). Since 0 is already a convex combination of the vertices, its row follows as well. Thus the prescribed gradients, values, switches and chronology all come from the one static function. No unvisited coordinate is assumed to remain inactive without this check.

### 3.3 Exact last-iterate payoff and original radius

At the final point, the gradient is \(w_m\) and its support is \(A_m-\bar P+S\). Consequently

\[
2F_K(X_n)=2S+1+A_m-2\bar P.
\]

This gives the actual full-word witness

\[
W_n(h)\ge
\frac{2S+1+A_m-2\bar P}
{\sum_i P_i^2/A_i-\bar P^2}.
\tag{17}
\]

The numerator is positive: (12) implies
\(\bar P\le S+1\), hence it is at least \(A_m-1>0\). The minimum is unique, so the radius in (15) has no hidden minimum-cone correction.

This construction applies to any chosen checkpoints exceeding 1, uses every step of the original word, allows zero steps within phases, and allows arbitrarily large finite steps. The dimension is \(m-1\). Parameters and the partition can depend on the entire word before the single function is selected.

### 3.4 What this replacement did and did not resolve

The negative cross products in (13) are realized by explicit vectors. The reduction of radius in (15) is a true orthogonal centering, with a unique valid minimizer. This removes the need to postulate an independent cheap support state or to add a separate positive clock coordinate in this family.

The decisive closure inequality is still unproved. I did not establish that, for every word for which both baselines in (1) are below \((1-\epsilon)/B_n\), there is a partition and parameters satisfying (12) for which (17) reaches that target with a word-independent loss. Backward existence of parameters in (12) alone does not control (15) sharply enough. Nor was a counterfamily of surviving words proved to exclude all such simplex choices together with the baselines. Therefore this family has not been declared insufficient on the basis of an already easy Silver word.

No interpolation or construction lemma is missing inside (12)--(17). The missing statement is the original-scale all-word selection and quantitative comparison with the exact discrete \(C_n/B_n\) recurrences. Presenting its optimization as a new Bellman or feasibility problem would not prove it.

## 4. Final audit and status

Both substantive lines use actual finite-dimensional static functions, original GD chronology and explicit initial radii. Multiplying any constructed function by a prescribed \(L>0\) restores \(L\)-smoothness and leaves the normalized performance unchanged. The target normalization throughout is \(W=2R\).

The support recurrence was independently checked. The retained-height operation has a genuine improvement over discarding those heights and is locally tight at the Zhang--Jiang bridge with an explicitly realized child. It does not establish recursively available joint states on arbitrary words. The simplex line has explicit full-history interpolation and a unique minimizer, but lacks the required all-word payoff estimate.

There is no complete affirmative candidate, no rigorous full-class asymptotic separation, and no uniform \(o(1)\) to report. The original target remains OPEN. This one-call/two-line allocation ends here; no automatic renewal, verification submission, truth-store change or Goal change is made.

# Fresh R5 direct closure attempt: select compatible histories using the full word

Project gd-exact-profile-20260926, original Goal ACTIVE/OPEN. ONE NEW gpt-6-astra/ultra native analytical invocation, consult OFF, up to TWO substantive proof lines. Mathematical programs, numerical arrays/scans/solvers and formal submissions ZERO. Parent owns ledger. Read current OPERATOR.md and COMPLETE PROBLEM.md. The user's latest priority is direct closure using Zhang-Jiang's proof, not more equivalent formulations or fixed-horizon toy models. All prior grants are CLOSED.

## Exact objective

Prove for every epsilon>0 a threshold N_epsilon independent of h such that for all n>=N_epsilon and ALL h in[0,infinity)^n,

    W_n(h)=sup 2(f_n-fstar)/(L||x0-xstar||^2)>=(1-epsilon)/B_n.

The exact benchmark is C0=B0=1, C_n=max_(k+l=n-1)Omega(C_k,C_l), B_n=max_(k+l=n-1)G(C_k,B_l), with every integer split, Omega(x,y)=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2 and G(x,y)=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2. W=2R. Original finite dimension, attained minimum, selected minimum/radius, every nonnegative/zero/large step and actual last iterate are mandatory. A genuine full-class asymptotic separation is an acceptable opposite answer if proved, but no restricted upper is one.

## Concrete starting object, not an abstract Bellman oracle

Read the complete final zhang_jiang_direct_closure_20261008_r4/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md (SHAF17DA7D70E5A2A23F7570380CF00FE6264E70B48913E0DA9814F8AE9385E1239) and HANDOFF.md. The main agent fully read them and closed that one-call/two-line allocation. They give actual static convex functions with all old and cross-history constraints paid, not accepted facts.

The available zero-extra-radius metric cap has a moment interval that excludes a maximum-step cut under the exhibited single-negative-lobe prefix test. Do not merely repeat that spectral construction or assume a universal split. The frozen-clock construction has a real ceiling on its specified quadratic-Silver child family, but that family is NOT a survivor of every alternative baseline; global Huber/quadratic witnesses were not included in that exclusion. Do not treat it as exclusion of a larger witness pool.

The primary proposed line is to choose child histories jointly using the WHOLE prescribed word, then use the exact R4 hull pasting on words not already covered by a global baseline. Source: Zhang-Jiang v2 https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2 Sections2.2/3.1/3.2. Their joint inequalities motivate tracking all required boundary information, but do not prove a converse or rank-one completeness.

Read SUPPORT_CLOCK_RECURRENCE_20261008_R5.md. It specializes the ACTUAL R4 hull construction and tracks its true initial support M, initial radius and terminal value exactly. Independently derive it before use. This may permit recursive pasting of nonquadratic children, unlike the already screened fixed quadratic children. M1 and M2 in the full two-child construction are actual maxima over the retained history, not free scalar parameters. The suffix can know the whole word before the function is fixed, but the function cannot change during its run.

## Closure work required

Try to prove the original inequality using a portfolio of ACTUAL witnesses with a proven selection rule. A global Huber handles W>=1/(1+2sum h); a scalar quadratic handles its exact polynomial value. Neither their completeness nor any claimed all-word decomposition is a premise. On surviving words, specify and realize the additional histories, control their support costs, select the cuts and parameters, and sum every loss against the exact C_n/B_n recurrence. No finite-horizon table is requested.

Your first substantive line should make the available static pasting quantitative on the surviving words, with one explicitly realizable recursively stable family if possible. If a second line is needed, it should remove a specific necessary loss or incompatible-history premise, not rename the same recurrence. Do not assume arbitrary near-extremal child histories can extend: the R4 unit-curvature example refutes that. Selection may depend on the entire known word and on the parent boundary data; prove that the selected children are actually available.

A successful deliverable is a complete target-level proof or a concrete chain reaching the exact uniform estimate, not just a favorable-looking state recursion or another equivalent feasibility condition. If a proposed reachable-state or decomposition lemma is needed, prove it; otherwise state the exact gap and do not call it routine or a new result. If the entire attempted family has an intrinsic loss on words where the BASELINE POOL also fails, show that precise scope and stop that line; a failure on an already easy Silver word alone is not enough to reject the whole combined strategy.

No unchanged proof is reopened by tuning a few cap parameters. No unproved uniformity, closest-minimum normalization, actual chronology or old cross rows may be dropped. A formal Gram direction is not an actual function.

## Evidence and bounded close

Only text/primary-source reads, manual reasoning, Markdown and hashes. No arithmetic/recurrence/symbolic programs, calculators, numerical files, new models/agents, paid consults, installations, worker/service actions, private memory reads, truth-store edits, publication, finalization, revocation, shutdown or Goal mutation. One invocation/<=TWO substantive lines/program0/formal0. Write self-contained PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in this new directory, mark all new math UNVERIFIED, then STOP. Parent closes ledger. No automatic renewal; original target remains OPEN unless its complete resolution is actually proved and later accepted.

# C112: one bounded joint-variable primitive-seed pilot

Status: UNVERIFIED research derivation and numerical discovery plan. No formal
gate is allocated. The original target remains open. Accepted mathematical
inputs are 04219554e41d9750 (Sections 10--13), 1252b8823bb5a290
(interpolation orientation), and 4c92d3fc4eb6153e (conditional thresholds/lift).
No shared draft is used as a mathematical premise.

## Full original model and the exact reduction

For a physical length r, keep every h_i>=0, 0<=i<r, and every ordered
Lambda_ij>=0, 0<=i,j<=r, i!=j; put Lambda_ii=0. Define s=sum h_i,
A=1+s, e=ones(r+1), T_ii=1, T_it=h_t for t<i, and zero otherwise.
Write L=diag(Lambda e)-Lambda, omega=(-2h_0,...,-2h_(r-1),2s)^T,
D=diag(h_i(2-h_i),-s(s+2)), and M=D+(LT+T^T L^T)/2.
The original constraints are L^T e=omega and M PSD on all R^(r+1).

The accepted original identity is V_h+sum Lambda_ij Q_ij=<M,Gram(g)>.
Here Q_ij=f_i-f_j+<g_i,x_j-x_i>+||g_i-g_j||^2/2<=0 by
1252b8823bb5a290, x_i=x_0-sum_(t<i)h_t g_t, and
V_h=2sum h_i(f_i-f_r)+sum h_i(1-h_i)||g_i||^2-s(s+1)||g_r||^2.
In a direct coefficient audit the value coefficient is canceled by the flow;
the position terms are sym(L(T-I)); the gradient-difference terms are
sym(L)-diag(omega)/2. Addition of V_h's diagonal gives exactly M.
This is the full original coordinate certificate, not an actual-gradient
quotient. Every zero-step coordinate and every ordered multiplier survives.

Here is the joint algebra allowing a smaller factor. Since Le=0, the sum
of the flow residuals is zero: e^T(L^T e-omega)=0. Thus the first r flow
equations imply the terminal equation. Put H_i=sum_(t<i)h_t. Then
(Te)_i=1+H_i, including H_r=s, and under the flow equations

  e^T D e=-sum h_i^2-s^2,
  e^T L T e=omega^T T e=2s^2-2sum h_i H_i
             =s^2+sum h_i^2,

because 2sum h_i H_i=s^2-sum h_i^2. Consequently e^T M e=0.
For a PSD symmetric M this forces Me=0: for any vector z, positivity of
(e+t z)^T M(e+t z) for both signs of real t makes the linear coefficient
2 z^T Me vanish. Conversely, if the first r entries of Me vanish,
e^T M e=0 forces its last entry to vanish as well.

Let P denote the leading r-by-r principal block of M, let u=ones(r),
and let B be the (r+1)-by-r matrix formed by stacking I_r above -u^T.
For symmetric M with Me=0, its last column is (-Pu,u^T P u)^T,
so M=B P B^T. In particular M PSD iff P PSD: one implication is
principal restriction, and the other follows from this identity.

Every real PSD P has P=K K^T for a lower triangular real K with
nonnegative diagonal, including singular P. A proof by induction:
if P_00=0 then positivity of its two-coordinate restrictions forces
P_0j=0 for all j; put the first column of K equal to zero and factor
the remaining PSD principal block. If P_00>0, set K_00=sqrt(P_00)
and K_i0=P_i0/K_00. The Schur complement is PSD, since its quadratic
form is that of P on (-P_00^(-1)P_0,rest z,z); factor it inductively.
This argument imposes neither strict diagonal positivity nor a rank cap.

Hence the joint polynomial model is exactly

  (L^T e-omega)_i=0                      for i<r,
  (Me)_i=0                              for i<r,
  M_ij-(K K^T)_ij=0                     for 0<=i<=j<r,
  h_i>=0, Lambda_ij>=0, K_ii>=0.

It has r+r(r+1)+r(r+1)/2 variables and 2r+r(r+1)/2 equalities.
Full M=B K K^T B^T is read back from the original model; the dropped
terminal equations are explicitly reported. All retained expressions have
degree at most two jointly in h,Lambda,K. Finite numerical boxes below
are additional search restrictions, never asserted global bounds.

## Independent calibration with a nonzero remainder

Use r=1, let h vary in [3/4,5/4], and maximize h. Set a=Lambda_01,
b=Lambda_10. Flow gives b=a+2h. Direct multiplication then gives

  M_00=h(2-h)+a(1-h),
  M_01=-a(1-h/2)-h(1-h),
  M_11=a-h^2,
  (Me)_0=h(1-a/2).

Since h>0 the null equation forces a=2, b=2+2h, and
M=(2-h^2)[[1,-1],[-1,1]]. Thus the exact constrained maximum is
h=5/4, a=2, b=9/2, K=sqrt(7)/4. Its remainder is nonzero.
The exact start h=1,a=2,b=4,K=1 is feasible. This calibration exercises
the same joint polynomial construction, full readback, and monitored
InteriorPoint solver intended for the two physical lengths.

## Independently justified full-rank warm-start words

For either r=5 or r=6 take h_i=1 for every physical i. The following
explicit array, used only to justify/start the word, is feasible:

  Lambda_(i,i+1)=2(i+1)^2,
  Lambda_(i+1,i)=2(i+1)(i+2),  0<=i<r,
  all other ordered entries=0.

All entries are nonnegative. At each nonterminal node, out-in=-2;
at the terminal node it is 2r. With T the lower triangular all-ones
matrix, direct row multiplication gives (LT)_ii=2i(i+1) for i<r,
(LT)_rr=2r(r+1), (LT)_(i,i+1)=-2(i+1)^2, and every other off-diagonal
entry zero. Adding D and symmetrizing gives

  M=sum_(i=0)^(r-1)(i+1)^2 (e_i-e_(i+1))(e_i-e_(i+1))^T.

Here e_i are the original coordinate unit columns. This PSD matrix
has kernel precisely span(e), since zero quadratic form means all
successive coordinates agree. Its leading principal block is positive
definite. Thus the original h=(1,...,1) word has a genuinely feasible
full original certificate and a nonzero remainder of maximal possible
rank r. The optimization model will NOT retain the path support or
the unit-step restrictions: all original h and Lambda become free.

## Fixed choices before reservation

Use existing Wolfram Language only; no installation. One calibration,
at most one fixed-word CSDP warm start per r, and at most one FindMinimum
InteriorPoint run per r. The warm-start SDP maximizes the least eigenvalue
of the leading principal block, with all original equations, 0<=Lambda<=400,
and an auxiliary 0<=tau<=100. Use its unmodified point only if the original
flow/null residuals are <=1e-8, all signs are nonnegative, and the principal
block is positive definite. Otherwise use the exact array just displayed;
this predefined fallback involves no extra solver and no jitter.

For joint runs use the boxes 0<=h<=6, 0<=Lambda<=400, |K_ij|<=100,
and 0<=K_ii<=100. Maximize A=1+sum h. No factor diagonal is required
strictly positive. Method InteriorPoint, machine precision, AccuracyGoal 8,
PrecisionGoal 8, at most150 iterations and2000 monitored objective/constraint
evaluation callbacks. Each callback logs its index, iteration, elapsed time,
capacity and residual/sign summary; each iteration saves the full variable
vector. Callback cap and wall deadline terminate through Catch/Throw;
MaxIterations and TimeConstrained provide additional guards.

Each job's exact source and metadata must be persisted and atomically
reserved before evaluation. The ledger charges the ENTIRE job cap as a
conservative guard, never refunds it, and reports measured kernel time
separately. Calibration/warm-start/reconstruction caps are10s each; each
joint run cap is40s. Kernel work is guarded below that cap, with separate
export/local-persistence allowance included. The total six-job maximum is
120s. A prior-work700s GUARD, not claimed measured time, enforces the900s
global backstop. Raw output is saved before any result summary.

Stop discovery immediately if a candidate has A greater than the exact
accepted Gamma5=7977607/806000 or Gamma6=387153999/32240000 and passes
the full original numerical readback at tolerance1e-7. The convenient
targets are A>=99/10 and A>=1201/100. Numerical feasibility is not a seed
proof. Only the single reserved10s job may reconstruct/read back exactly.
No formal gate, extra run, restart, global bound or nonexistence claim.

## Methodological reference and retrieval record

Das Gupta, Van Parys, Ryu, *Branch-and-Bound Performance Estimation
Programming: A Unified Methodology for Constructing Optimal Optimization
Methods*, arXiv:2203.07305v5, Section3.1.2 and Algorithm1, are methodology
only. Their variables describe a different PEP. This pilot adapts the
fixed-method SDP start followed by joint Cholesky-variable local optimization.
No paper theorem is a correctness premise, and Stage3 branch-and-bound is
not implemented. The factor equivalence needed here is proved above.

The primary PDF was downloaded. Local PDF extraction tools were absent;
the browser PDF request failed. The primary HTML was downloaded and its
text extracted with the standard-library HTML parser; the specified sections
were read. Matlas returned unrelated truncated-total-least-squares and
condition-number results; none is imported.

Official documentation read before evaluation:
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/ref/FindMinimum.html
https://reference.wolfram.com/language/tutorial/ConstrainedOptimizationLocalNumerical.html
The latter states that EvaluationMonitor is invoked for objective and
equality/inequality evaluations and StepMonitor once per interior-point step.
The required Wolfram context tool was asked for documentation only; its
automatic Alpha queries returned 'No Results Found'. No such query evaluated
a word or supplies mathematical evidence.

## Length-five start update before its reservation

Latest guidance 0fdd0f1dbfd2b8f1 offers shared draft8ce8b65121586185 as a
warm-start lead. The following reproduces the needed identity rather than
using the draft as a mathematical premise. It replaces ONLY the previously
planned unit-word length-five start, before that job is reserved. Length six
keeps the unit-word start. No additional solve or screen is introduced.

Fix t=9/10, a=sqrt(2), b=(-a+sqrt(10+8a))/2, p=a+b, lambda=1+a,
X=t a,Y=t b,C=1+X+Y,delta=1/10,d=(a-1)C-delta. The identity
b^2+ab=2lambda gives pb=2lambda. Put c=(C+d)/(C-d), k=1+2c,
Delta=d(2C+d)-C^2=-delta(2a C-delta), tau=-Delta/(C-d), eta=2tau,
u=2(X+Y)(1+X)/Y, v=2(X+Y)C/Y. The actual start is

  h=(X,Y,1+d,X,Y),
  Lambda=
   [[0,2,0,0,0,0],
    [2(1+X),0,u,0,0,0],
    [0,v,0,0,0,2cd+eta],
    [0,0,2cX,0,2k,2cX],
    [0,0,2cY,2k(1+X),0,ku+2cY],
    [0,0,2c+eta,0,kv,0]].

Every entry is nonnegative. To justify signs without numerical screening,
7/5<a<3/2 and the polynomial b^2+ab-2lambda at 3-a is 7-5a<0,
so p>3, C>5/2. Consequently d>1/2,0<d<C and C-d>C/2. Thus
c,k,u,v,tau,eta are positive. Using v-u=2(C-1) and c(C-d)=C+d,
the six divergences of the displayed array are respectively
-2X,-2Y,-2(1+d),-2X,-2Y,2(2C+d-1). This is the original flow.

For completeness, direct multiplication of the original child T,L,D for
word(X,Y) and array [[0,2,0],[2(1+X),0,u],[0,v,0]] yields
M_child=w0(g0-g1)^2+w1(g1-g2)^2, with
w0=2-X^2=2(1-t)(1+t),
w1=((X+Y)/Y)[2-Y^2+(2-Y)X]=2(p/b)(1-t)(1+lambda t).
The diagonal is (w0,w0+w1,w1), off-diagonal entries -w0,-w1,0;
these are obtained from M_00=2-X^2,M_12=(v(Y-1)-u)/2,
M_22=-(X+Y)(X+Y+2)+v and the full flow/null identities.

Here is also the parent coefficient audit. Let P=2,Z=5 and
E=X Q_(3,P)+Y Q_(4,P)+Q_(Z,P)+d Q_(P,Z)+X Q_(3,Z)+Y Q_(4,Z).
Its value part is 2X f3+2Y f4+(d-C)fP+(2-C-d)fZ;
its gradient part is X(1-X)||g3||^2+Y(1-Y)||g4||^2+
[(C-d)/2-d^2]||gP||^2+(C+d)||gZ||^2/2.
All mixed products cancel by substituting the actual GD positions.
Subtracting the two child energies from the parent and using
c Delta=k d^2-C^2 gives

 V_parent=V_left+k V_right-2c E+c Delta(||gZ||^2-||gP||^2).

The displayed array embeds the two child arrays, adds2c E and adds
eta to both (P,Z),(Z,P). With z=gP-gZ,J=X(g3-gZ)+Y(g4-gZ), direct
substitution gives Q_(P,Z)+Q_(Z,P)=-d||z||^2-(C+d)<z,gZ>-<z,J>.
The full parent remainder is therefore

 M=w0||g0-g1||^2+w1||g1-g2||^2
     +k[w0||g3-g4||^2+w1||g4-g5||^2]
     +tau[(C-d)||z||^2-2<z,J>].

This identity includes every original gradient and mixed entry. To prove
PSD, write U=g3-g4,V=g4-g5, R=X^2/w0+(X+Y)^2/w1,
q=tau/(C-d), and sigma=k-qR. Then
R=(1-t)^(-1)t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)]. The bracketed
expression multiplied by t^2 is increasing for t>=0 by differentiation,
so at t=9/10 it is at most lambda/2. Hence

 (C-d)^2 sigma
  =(3C+d)(C-d)-(2a C-delta)t^2[1/(1+t)+lambda/(1+lambda t)]
  >(3/2)C^2-a lambda C >(15/4)C-(7/2)C=C/4>0.

The exact full sum of squares is

 w0||g0-g1||^2+w1||g1-g2||^2+sigma(w0||U||^2+w1||V||^2)
 +q||(X+Y)sqrt(w0/w1)U-X sqrt(w1/w0)V||^2
 +tau(C-d)||z-J/(C-d)||^2.

Indeed R(w0||U||^2+w1||V||^2)-||XU+(X+Y)V||^2 is exactly the
fourth squared norm before its q factor. All displayed weights are
positive. Its kernel is precisely the common-gradient line, proving
positive definiteness of the leading principal block. This reproduces
the fixed start's original signs, flow, and full PSD jointly from the
accepted primitive identity. It makes no improving-seed claim about this
start or the shared one-dimensional family. The local solve frees every
h,Lambda and factor coordinate, with no relation to t enforced.

The calibration reserved job1 matched its exact endpoint with maximum
retained-equation residual1.1026235480215973e-11,10 iterations and30
monitored callbacks. The raw tool emitted Unset::norep and a graphics
message outside its exported kernel message list; both are preserved.
Subsequent source uses independent atomic symbols instead of indexed
expressions as decision variables to avoid unnecessary localization
messages; the polynomial equations and method are unchanged.

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-002545 -->
# C112 bounded joint-variable pilot: no credible seed

The ONE round authorized by TASK.md and guidance81b4e3cbbe96d65a is
complete. Neither local run produced a credible sufficient-capacity
primitive seed. The original exact-profile problem is OPEN. No fact_submit,
formal verification, installation, paid consult, native subagent, publication,
finalization, revocation, shutdown, or extra solver run occurred.

All new mathematical derivations are UNVERIFIED author drafts. Numerical
calibration and readbacks are evidence about this implementation, not a fact
graph acceptance. The accepted inputs remain04219554e41d9750,
1252b8823bb5a290, and4c92d3fc4eb6153e.

## Model and starts

The complete joint derivation is model_and_plan.md. It rederives the original
V_h+sum Lambda Q identity, proves the flow-implied e^T M e=0 identity,
and proves equivalence of the original full PSD requirement to first-r flow
and null equations plus the entire leading-block lower-Cholesky identity.
The lifting basis B=[I_r;-ones(r)^T] gives M=B K K^T B^T. Zero factor
diagonals and every possible PSD rank are allowed. No original gradient
coordinate or ordered multiplier is deleted, including at zero steps.

The search moves every original h and every ordered Lambda jointly with
all lower-factor variables. The only extra restrictions are the explicitly
registered finite search boxes: h in[0,6], Lambda in[0,400], and each lower
factor entry in[-100,100] with nonnegative diagonal. These are not global
bounds. No symmetry, support, unit-step, repeated-gradient, zero-remainder,
or positive-rank restriction is imposed on a joint run.

The r=1 calibration maximizes h in[3/4,5/4]. Its independently derived
exact endpoint is h=5/4,Lambda01=2,Lambda10=9/2,
M=(7/16)[[1,-1],[-1,1]],K=sqrt(7)/4. The numerical endpoint had
maximum retained-equation residual1.1026235480215973e-11 and matched
this reference within the registered tolerance. There were10 step callbacks
and30 evaluation callbacks. Raw evaluator warnings are preserved.

For length5 the actual start is the fixed t=9/10 interior point suggested
by the max2 shared lead8ce8b65121586185. Its full signs, flow, and positive
rank-five remainder are reproduced jointly in model_and_plan.md; the draft
lead is not used as a correctness premise. The physical start is approximately

  (1.2727922061357855,1.4411086432670979,2.4383481011316324,
   1.2727922061357855,1.4411086432670979),
  A=8.866149799937398.

The actual length6 start is six unit steps, with the explicitly justified
path-Laplacian remainder and capacity7. Its principal Cholesky factor is
lower bidiagonal, with diagonal1,...,6 and subdiagonal-1,...,-5.
All other factor entries are zero. These sparsities describe starts only.

Each of the two permitted fixed-word CSDP solves attempted to improve the
principal eigenvalue margin with all ordered multipliers available. Both
outputs failed the preregistered original sign/residual test: some multipliers
were negative at approximately8e-7 in magnitude. Both used the predeclared
analytic anchor, with no clipping, PSD jitter, new word, or extra solve.
The selected length5 principal spectrum was positive, with least value
0.13375177387703158; length6 had least value0.558844956465161.

## Actual local runs

Both runs used one Wolfram Language15.0.1 FindMinimum invocation with
Method InteriorPoint, machine precision, at most150 iterations and2000
evaluation callbacks, plus the registered wall guards. Length5 used
AccuracyGoal/PrecisionGoal8. After observing its convergence trouble,
length6 used goals10 and the bijective variable scaling Lambda=10*l,
K=3*k. Every original equation, box, sign, and final readback stayed the
same under that positive scaling; no equality was rescaled or weakened.

| Observed quantity | length5 | length6 |
|---|---:|---:|

The final length5 physical word was

For length5 the solver warning says it did not converge in150 iterations,

## Accounting, transport, and artifacts

The new owner-only ledger is CLOSED: five jobs and110 accounted seconds.
The sum of reported kernel elapsed times is8.998130 seconds. The110-second
number charges every entire reserved cap as a conservative guard with no
refunds; it is not claimed measured time. The separate prior-work guard
is700 seconds, also not claimed actual elapsed, giving810 guarded seconds
against the900-second global backstop. Old ledgers were not modified.

The calibration's30 callbacks plus the two joint runs'698 callbacks give
728 logged nonlinear evaluation callbacks. The fixed-word CSDP interface
does not expose corresponding iteration/callback counts; they are marked
unavailable, not fabricated as zero work. Every job includes exactly one
solver invocation. Local raw-decoding/persistence timings and conservative
export/local guards are recorded per outcome. No external timeout occurred.
The unused sixth job and its10 seconds are retired. Both allowed joint
lengths have been used; this round is not self-renewing.

Before every evaluator call, the exact request and metadata were written
and the job atomically reserved with SHA256 hashes. Every actual raw tool
response was saved before parsing or summary. The initial reservation helper
incorrectly expected the initial ledger status to be 'open'; the prepared
ledger was 'allocated_not_started'. This caused a bookkeeping assertion
before any evaluation. The helper was corrected to preserve the original
schema/counters and then reserved normally. No job was refunded or reset.

The verbose warm5 output was actually truncated by the tool. Its complete
returned SDP arrays and complete selected start vector survived; the missing
part is not called an observed complete warm5 readback. The already allocated

unchanged vector. The recovered record is explicitly labeled as a later
joint5 readback, not identical lost floating output. No recovery solver or
additional mathematical evaluation occurred.

For joint5, warm6 and joint6, lossless gzip/base64 chunks carried compact
JSON. Every numbered chunk was received and decoded. Both joint callback
histories are COMPLETE, not sampled. No cloud publication or external log
upload was used. The final and best original h,Lambda,M,K,B, all original
flow/null/factor/equality residuals, all original sign booleans, full and
principal spectra, objective histories, warnings, version, and counters
are in the decoded JSON files. Their raw encoded responses are also saved.

Key files in this public directory:

* model_and_plan.md: complete joint derivations and the fixed-start audit.

Guidance0fdd0f1dbfd2b8f1 was read before choosing/reserving warm5. Later
guidancebd2c492e4fe76d41 was read at closeout, after the joint calls. The
already chosen lossless compact transport, original-model readback in joint5,
and preserved warnings agree with it. No claim that it was read earlier is
made. Current TASK remains the one-round allocation.

## What the bounded outcome supports

This pilot supplies no actual sufficient-capacity certificate and no seed
proof. The accepted physical lift at18*2^j-6 or21*2^j-7 is therefore not
triggered. No conclusion about the unrestricted cone, global optimality,
nonexistence, or the original asymptotic minimax problem follows.

The concrete issue exposed here is feasibility convergence of the local
joint factor model: both nonlinear runs exhausted their iteration allowance
while the full matrix equalities remained too inaccurate, and both warm SDPs
also had sign/residual failures. A justified future direction, only with a
new allocation, is to repair that interface before further discovery—for
example, test an exact parametrization of the linear-in-Lambda flow/null
equations with all degeneracies retained, followed by a fully metered PSD
factor optimization. This is a proposal, not an established lossless
parametrization or an inference that the threshold can be reached. No further
run, analysis of subthreshold families, or formal gate is launched here.

Methodological sources, used only for implementation:

and official RawJSON/GZIP/BaseEncode documentation for transport.

This lower bound, coupled to the unchanged first-radius formula(43),
